<details><summary>Prior position — Phase 37 plan 37-02 COMPLETE (2/4) — POST /migrate routes (the move logic)</summary>

**Phase 37 plan 37-02 COMPLETE — 2/4 plans — "QB → NetSuite migration walkthrough — POST /migrate routes (the move logic)"** (Wave 2, autonomous, `requirements: [QbMigrationRoutes, RampMiniModule, MigrationAuditTrail]`). Repo: `/Users/jeet/turion-space-demo/`. **Replaced the 501-stub POST routes from 37-01 with the real atomic-transaction implementations.** `POST /api/quickbooks/:type/migrate` accepts `{qbIds: string[]}`, runs in a single `pool.connect()` BEGIN/COMMIT transaction, applies a per-type `applyMapping(client, t, qbId, qbRow)` async helper (6 branches: coa direct-copy 10-field / customer 12-field w/ BillAddr unnest / vendor 10-field w/ BillAddr unnest / item 13-field w/ 3 COA cross-refs / invoice 12-field w/ customer + item cross-refs + line-item nesting + embedded paymentRecorded on Balance==0 / bill 10-field w/ vendor + account cross-refs + line-item nesting + embedded paymentRecorded), INSERTs into `turion.<ns_table>` (special-cased `(id, name, source_data)` shape for `customers`; all others `(id, source_data)`), flips `turion.qb_records.status='migrated'` + sets `migration_run_id`, writes ONE `turion.migration_runs` row per batch (full run-summary jsonb: `{actor, run_type, run_id, started_at, finished_at, source_count, migrated_count, skipped_count, error_count, error_details, summary}`), writes ONE `turion.audit_log` CREATE row per migrated record. `POST /api/ramp/card-txns/migrate` mirrors the QB shape but with one record type (card_txn → bills), synchronous `applyRampMapping(rampId, rampRow)` helper (no cross-refs — vendor is hardcoded "Ramp · Corporate Card"), `paymentRecorded` always set (Ramp txns post-paid). **Cross-record refs resolved via the SAME pool client** so a batch that migrates customers BEFORE invoices sees the just-INSERTed customer rows. **Unresolved refs surface as `__warnings` array on the ns row** (best-effort + diagnostic; row still inserted — plan §truths). **Idempotency proven end-to-end:** re-migrating an already-migrated row returns it in `skipped[]` (no 409, no error_count increment). **Hardened catch** — 500s return only `{error: 'migration failed'}`, server-side `console.error('[quickbooks/migrate] ', e?.message)` for diagnostics (Phase 36-08 audit rule). **Exhaustiveness check** via `const _exhaustive: never = t` at the end of applyMapping — tsc fails if a 7th QbType is added without a branch. **Verification:** `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0; `npm run build` rebuilt `dist/` clean; ERP button audit 0 violations across 213 routes (unchanged from 37-01); **end-to-end smoke test against prod Supabase** (pulled DATABASE_URL from Lambda env var — `postgres.lbpkbpfwdpnwlccmlfxn@aws-1-us-east-2.pooler.supabase.com`): migrated `coa/1000` → `{migrated:[1000], skipped:[], errors:[]}`; re-migrated same id → `{migrated:[], skipped:[1000], errors:[]}` (idempotent); migrated `ramp/RMP-TXN-44012` → success; confirmed rows in `gl_accounts/1000` + `bills/RMP-RMP-TXN-44012` with mapped shapes (gl_accounts row had `name`, `type`, `subType`, `openingBalance`, etc.; ramp bill had `vendor:'Ramp · Corporate Card'`, `status:'Paid'`, `balance:0`, `paymentRecorded.method:'Ramp Card'`); confirmed 3 `migration_runs` rows; confirmed `audit_log` CREATE rows; confirmed `qb_records/1000.status='migrated'` + `migration_run_id` populated. **Smoke-test side effects rolled back** (deleted ns rows, reset source rows to 'new', deleted audit entries, deleted migration_runs) — DB returns to clean state for 37-03's wizard demo. **1 commit** on `turion-space-demo` main, `jeet-avatar <jm@techcloudpro.com>`: `d026f1e` (quickbooks.ts +365 lines / ramp.ts +112 lines / both dist/*.js rebuilt). **NOT pushed, NOT deployed** (37-04 owns it). **2 deviations** auto-fixed: [Rule 3 — blocker] dropped `updated_at=now()` from the Ramp UPDATE because `turion.ramp_card_txns` has no `updated_at` column (only `qb_records` does) — would have thrown at first POST; <details><summary>Prior position — Phase 36 plan 36-07 COMPLETE (7/9) — in-tree WIP resolved + plaintext Resend key scrubbed</summary>

**Phase 36 plan 36-07** (Wave 3, autonomous — resolve the long-stashed in-tree WIP in `turion-space-demo` + scrub a plaintext Resend API key; depended on 36-02+36-05; in `/Users/jeet/turion-space-demo/`) — COMPLETE. (1) **Security fix:** `backend/src/routes/notify.ts` had a hardcoded plaintext Resend API key (`re_JRdox6wH_…`) in `Authorization: Bearer …` — replaced with a lazy, memoized `getResendKey()` reading `process.env.RESEND_API_KEY` at *send* time (not module load → never touches the Lambda cold-start path); missing key ⇒ `console.log('[notify] Resend not configured …')` and an early `return` (no throw, no crash; `/api/notify/visit` still `{ok:true}`s instantly). Mirrors `turion-satellite`'s `assistant.ts` `getApiKey()` pattern. (2) **Cold-start fix in `agents.ts`** (Rule-1 auto-fix the plan's Task 3 anticipated): removed the module-top `if (!process.env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY) throw …` + top-level `new Anthropic(…)` (both ran at module load = on the cold-start path) → replaced with a lazy `getAnthropic()` getter; the two `anthropic.messages.create(…)` call sites now `getAnthropic().messages.create(…)` ⇒ a missing `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` makes the `/api/agents/*` routes return `{error:"ANTHROPIC_API_KEY not configured"}` instead of crashing the Lambda. (3) **Landed the agents feature (NOT reverted)** — `backend/src/routes/agents.ts`: 3 agents (NCR→CAPA closure, EVMS Watchdog, Integration Sentinel) + a shared `runAgentLoop()`, all writing real rows to `turion.*` tables + `turion.audit_log`; `agent-sales-cash.html` (4 "▶ Run Agent" buttons → `POST /api/agents/{run,ncr-capa,evms,integration-sentinel}` — routes confirmed against the router); `dashboard-cio.html` (+~252 lines); `about-this-demo.html` (presentation redesign). (4) **Rebuilt `dist/` from cleaned `src/`** (`npm run build`) — the leaked key is in **neither `src/` nor `dist/`** (`grep -rn "re_JRdox6wH" backend/` → 0); `dist/app.js` now also reflects 36-02's `lookups` router + 36-05's `src` changes (it's the canonical build of the committed `src/app.ts` — correct). <details><summary>Prior position — Phase 36 plan 36-06 COMPLETE (6/9) — ERP view-page persistence: ns-editable.js → PATCH</summary>

Plan 36-06 (Wave 3, autonomous — ERP view-page persistence: `ns-editable.js` → PATCH; depended on 36-04+36-05; in `/Users/jeet/turion-space-demo/`) — COMPLETE. (1) **`ns-editable.js` reworked from a pure-localStorage editor into a hybrid**: an in-place "Edit mode" cell (or its nearest ancestor — `host = el.closest('[data-edit-module][data-edit-entity][data-edit-id]')`) can now declare `data-edit-module` (salesforce|netsuite|arena|mes) / `data-edit-entity` (route segment, e.g. `sales-orders`, `items`, `ncrs`, `capas`, `stages`) / `data-edit-id` (record id; for `mes/stages` it's the stage number) / `data-edit-path` (the `source_data` key — dotted paths build a nested `{a:{b:val}}` body so the backend's `{...before, ...req.body}` merge keeps deep keys) / `data-edit-type="number"` (coerce). On blur, a backend-target cell `fetch`es `PATCH ${API_BASE}/api/<module>/<entity>/<id>` → on success the new value becomes `data-edit-original` (the baseline) + `nsToast('Saved · …')`; **on failure the cell reverts to the original** (no silent edit loss) + an error toast. Cells WITHOUT `data-edit-*` keep the original `localStorage[editable:…]` demo behavior (zero markup change). `restoreValues()` skips backend-backed cells — the DB (via `data-loader.js` / re-render on `turion-data-ready`) is the source of truth, never shadowed by stale localStorage. `API_BASE = (window.TURION_CONFIG && window.TURION_CONFIG.API_BASE) || '<literal>'` per 36-02. (2) **`data-edit-*` cells added** to the well-mapped handful: `netsuite-customer-so.html` SO ns-form (`netsuite`/`sales-orders`/`SO-2026-0341`) — Order Date→`soDate`, Contract Type→`contractType`; `netsuite-items.html` spec ns-form (`netsuite`/`items`/`ADCS-RW-MEDIUM-A`) — Description→`description`, Mass→`mass`[number]. These ns-form `.value` cells are plain text (no child elements → editable) and map 1:1 to a record id printed on the page; keys match the `SO_DATA`/`ITEM_DATA` `source_data` shapes `/api/data/all` serves. (3) **Primary state-change buttons wired**: `mes-shop-floor.html` stage modal got a "▶ Mark Stage N in progress" button → `PATCH /api/mes/stages/:num` `{status:'in_progress', statusLabel:'In progress'}` (built in the `inner.innerHTML` template, attached via `addEventListener` after render — no inline `onclick`); `arena-qms.html` NCR/CAPA detail modal got a "✓ Close NCR/CAPA <id>" button → `PATCH /api/arena/{ncrs,capas}/:id` `{status:'Closed'}` (`showQmsModal()` gained an optional `recordRef` arg; `showNcr` passes `{entity:'ncrs',…}`, `showCapa` `{entity:'capas',…}`; button via `addEventListener`). Each button: `disabled`+"Saving…" → "✓ Saved/Closed" / revert+`nsToast` on error. **All PATCH routes used already existed** (`netsuite.ts` keyedEntity `/sales-orders` & `/items`; `mes.ts` `r.patch('/stages/:num')`; `arena.ts` keyedEntity `/ncrs` & `/capas`) — **no `backend/src` or `backend/dist` change, nothing rebuilt; `backend/dist/app.js` (36-07's shared WIP) untouched.** **Left read-only / simulated (pragmatic scope, per the plan's "no dead ends on PRIMARY actions, not every read-only cell is a DB write" mandate):** `salesforce-account.html` (its Edit/Follow/Sharing/Convert-to-NS-SO buttons stay `nsToast()` demos; its `sf-field`/`meta-item` value cells all have `<a>` children so aren't editable + map to a single hardcoded USSF account — it does include `ns-editable.js` so future `data-edit-*` cells will Just Work; Convert-to-SO would need a real conversion endpoint); ECO submit/approve (no `PATCH /api/arena/ecos/:id` exists — `ecos` is not a `keyedEntity`, only `ecos-create` POST; adding an `/ecos` keyedEntity + table is a backend follow-up, 36-07 owns `backend/*` WIP); NetSuite invoice/JE "post" (`PATCH /api/netsuite/{invoices,journal-entries}/:id` exists but no "post this JE/invoice" UI control exists on the view pages — `+ New Invoice`/`+ Submit deliverable` are `nsToast()` demos; `netsuite-setup.html`'s edit path `PATCH /api/extras/setup/:key` confirmed wired from 36-02, not re-touched); financial-statement pages (COA/TB/BS/FP&A), KPI rollup cards, the MES stage *grid* cells (vs the modal), and `ns-actions.js`'s "More Actions ▾" `confirm:`/`tab:`/`url:` simulated-confirm items (untouched). `node --check ns-editable.js` clean; `new Function(code)` syntax check over every non-`src` `<script>` of `arena-qms.html`(1)/`mes-shop-floor.html`(1)/`netsuite-items.html`(1)/`netsuite-customer-so.html`(0) → parse OK; local `http.server` smoke — `ns-editable.js` + all 4 view pages HTTP 200, each still refs `data-loader.js`+`turion-config.js`, the 3 ns-editable pages ref `ns-editable.js` (mes-shop-floor never did — its button is self-contained); `grep` confirms `ns-editable.js` carries the `method:'PATCH'` call, the 2 netsuite pages carry `data-edit-entity`/`data-edit-path`, mes/arena pages carry `method:'PATCH'` to `/api/mes/stages/` & `/api/arena/`, `git diff --stat ns-actions.js` empty (untouched), no new inline `onclick=` on touched HTMLs. 1 commit `1cca9b0` on `turion-space-demo` main under `jeet-avatar <jm@techcloudpro.com>` (`git -c user.email=… -c user.name=…`; On Save in create mode: `satelliteApi.post('/api/parts',{…})` → `satelliteApi.post('/api/satellites/'+satId+'/instances',{part_definition_id:pd.id})` → `satelliteApi.post('/api/satellites/'+satId+'/bom',{child_part_instance_id:inst.id,parent_part_instance_id,qty,ref_designator?})` → close + `r.toast('Part created and added to the BOM')` + reload; `409` on `POST /api/parts` highlights the field; partial-create failures tell the user the part exists + can be added manually (no silent half-create). Cancel to delete sub-lines first" → `del(...+'?recursive=1')`; reload+toast. requireAuth, hardened catch) — `queryOne('SELECT id,child_part_instance_id FROM bom_lines WHERE id=$1 AND satellite_id=$2')` → `404 {error:'BOM line not found'}` if no row (verifies the line belongs to this satellite); then `queryOne('SELECT COUNT(*)::int AS n FROM bom_lines WHERE parent_part_instance_id=$1 AND satellite_id=$2', [child_part_instance_id, satId])`; if `n>0` and NOT `?recursive=1` (also `recursive=true`) → `409 {error:'This line has child lines; pass ?recursive=1 to delete the subtree', child_line_count:n}`; if `n>0` and recursive → a `WITH RECURSIVE subtree` CTE collects this line + all descendant `bom_lines` (walk down via `parent_part_instance_id = st.child_part_instance_id`, `satellite_id=$2`), then `DELETE FROM bom_lines WHERE id = ANY($1::uuid[])` (`deleted_lines = ids.length`); if `n=0` → `DELETE FROM bom_lines WHERE id=$1` (`deleted_lines=1`); on success best-effort `audit_log` action `delete_bom_line` (`entity_type:'bom_line'`, payload `{satellite_id,recursive,deleted_lines}`) + `200 {ok:true,deleted_lines}` — **200 with a JSON body, never 204** (`satelliteApi.del()` calls `res.json()`); only `bom_lines` rows are deleted, never `part_instances` — the orphaned `part_instance` survives and `/bom/tree`'s roots CTE re-roots it. `/bom/tree` retired_at surfacing: `pd.retired_at` added to the roots-CTE select list, `c_pd.retired_at` to the recursive-child select, `retired_at` to the final outer SELECT + the `TreeNode` interface — **NO `retired_at IS NULL` filter** on the roots CTE or the recursive join (filtering there amputates sub-assemblies under a force-retired part — RESEARCH.md Pitfall 3); Editing model works on the LIVE SVG DOM: **Select** — click → `selectableTarget()` walks up to nearest `rect/circle/ellipse/line/polyline/polygon/path/text` (never into `.__editor-ui`), draws a dashed selection box + 8 resize handles + 1 rotate handle into a `<g class="__editor-ui">` appended to the SVG; click empty → deselect. **Move** — drag body → `transform="translate(dx dy) …existing…"` (`clientToSvg()` via `getScreenCTM().inverse()`); pointerup snapshots + redraws handles. **Resize** — drag a handle → scale-about-anchor matrix `translate(anchor) scale(sx,sy) translate(-anchor) …existing…` (anchor = opposite corner/edge; edge handles lock one axis). **Rotate** — drag rotate handle (above bbox center) → `transform="rotate(deg cx cy) …existing…"`. **Delete** — `Delete`/`Backspace` key (ignored while typing in an input) or the Delete button → `sel.remove()` + snapshot. **Edit text** — dbl-click a `<text>` → inline `<input>` at the click; Enter commits `textContent`, Esc cancels. **Add primitive** — a `+ *` button arms `pendingTool`; next click-drag on the canvas → `createElementNS` with sensible defaults (stroke/fill discovered from existing drawing elements else `#444`/`#cccccc`), appended to first `<g>` (or root `<svg>`); snapshot + auto-select. **Properties panel** — floating top-right, per-tag editable attrs (`x/y/width/height/cx/cy/r/rx/ry/x1..y2/points/d/fill/stroke/stroke-width/font-size/font-family/text-anchor/opacity`) + (for `<text>`) editable text content; `input`→live update, `change`→snapshot. **Undo/redo** — every committed mutation pushes `serialize()` (an `XMLSerializer` string with `.__editor-ui` + `data-svged-sel` stripped) onto `undoStack` (cap 50, clears `redoStack`); Undo/Redo re-parse the popped string, replace the canvas `<svg>`, re-wire pointer handlers; `Ctrl/Cmd+Z` / `+Shift+Z` / `+Y` bound. **Save** → `onSave(serialize())` (or textarea value in fallback) + close. **Cancel/Esc/backdrop-click** → `onCancel?.()` + close. **Revert** (only rendered if `onRevert` passed) → close + `onRevert()`. The module imports NOTHING — host-agnostic via callbacks; 35-05 wires `onSave`→`satelliteApi.patch('/api/parts/'+id+'/drawing',{drawing_svg})` and `onRevert`→`satelliteApi.post('/api/parts/'+id+'/drawing/regenerate',{})`. <details><summary>Prior position — Plan 33-01 (migration 020 — sales_orders + spawn_satellite_program()) COMPLETE</summary>

**Phase 33 IN PROGRESS (Wave 1) — end-to-end satellite-build flow.** Plan 33-01 (Wave 1, autonomous — migration 020: `turion_satellite.sales_orders` table + `satellites.sales_order_id` + `spawn_satellite_program()`, applied to prod) — COMPLETE. Created `/Users/jeet/turion-satellite/migrations/020_add_sales_orders_and_program_seed.sql` (idempotent idiom: `SET search_path TO turion_satellite, public;` + `DO $$ … current_database() NOT IN ('postgres') … RAISE EXCEPTION … $$;` guard + `BEGIN; … COMMIT;`). (1) `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS turion_satellite.sales_orders` — id uuid PK gen_random_uuid(), order_number text NOT NULL UNIQUE, customer_name text NOT NULL, program_name text NOT NULL, contract_value_usd numeric(14,2), status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open' CHECK in ('open','in_build','delivered','cancelled'), source_data jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb, satellite_id uuid REFERENCES turion_satellite.satellites(id) ON DELETE SET NULL, created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(). (2) `ALTER TABLE turion_satellite.satellites ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS sales_order_id uuid REFERENCES turion_satellite.sales_orders(id) ON DELETE SET NULL` + `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_satellites_sales_order ON … (sales_order_id) WHERE sales_order_id IS NOT NULL`. (3) `CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION turion_satellite.spawn_satellite_program(p_name text, p_designation text, p_sales_order_id uuid, p_actor uuid, p_template text DEFAULT 'standard-bus') RETURNS uuid LANGUAGE plpgsql` — body: rejects p_template ≠ 'standard-bus'; reads SAT-003/Cygnus `24587565-b15b-42ce-b590-87ecf9b6bb99` DYNAMICALLY (RAISE if absent); coerces p_actor to a real `team_members.id` or NULL (part_stage_events.actor_id FKs team_members); INSERTs the new `satellites` row (status='design', sales_order_id=p_sales_order_id, program_start=now()::date) RETURNING id; back-links the SO; clones `part_instances` (copies part_definition_id/instance_index/notes only — serial_number, created_by, the Phase-25 cross-system TEXT FK cols all stay NULL); rewires `bom_lines` via a CTE old→new id map joined on (part_definition_id, instance_index), LEFT JOIN on parent (root NULL preserved), INNER JOIN on child; seeds one `part_stage_events` row per new instance at stage code='drawing' (RAISE if missing), direction='forward', status='entered', actor_id=coerced actor; RETURNS the new satellite id. Function is intentionally NOT idempotent (each call = a new satellite); the migration DDL is. Applied to prod Postgres (`aws-1-us-east-2.pooler.supabase.com:6543/postgres`, secret `turion-satellite/production/database-url-NCbgX6`, `?…` query suffix stripped): first apply → SET / DO / BEGIN / CREATE TABLE / ALTER TABLE / CREATE INDEX / CREATE FUNCTION / COMMIT (0 ERRORs); `\d turion_satellite.sales_orders` shows 9 cols + the status CHECK + the FK both directions; `\df turion_satellite.spawn_satellite_program` shows the 5-arg function → uuid; `satellites.sales_order_id` uuid nullable=YES; `information_schema.tables` returns the sales_orders row. **Phase 32 IN PROGRESS (Wave 1) — build/procurement process documentation + cleanup.** Plan 32-01 (Wave 1, autonomous — part.html make/buy decision card + symmetric BUY procurement panel + fixed BUY workflow + [3d-wd] removal) — COMPLETE: edited `turion-space-demo/satellite/part.html` only. (1) Added `safeGet(path)` (404→null) + a `GET /api/make-buy-decisions/${satId}/${partId}` fetch wired only when `?sat=` present (404 = "undecided", no error toast); `effMakeBuy = (decision && decision.decision) || process.make_buy || part.default_make_buy` and the page-wide `mb` aliases it so the workflow visualizer / Make-Buy detail panel / cost panel / materials toggle all branch off the recorded decision. (2) New `#realizationPanel` ("Realization · how this part is built or procured" — standard `panel`/`panel-header`/`subtitle` chrome) with `renderRealization()`: decision present → MAKE/BUY tag (+ `decision_status` chip) · `escapeHtml(rationale)` · `decided by {decided_by_name} on {fmtDate(decided_at)}` · amber `re_evaluate` note linking to `cost-detail.html?sat=&part_inst=` (when the row carries `part_instance_id`) else `cost.html?sat=`; decision null but `?sat=` present → "Make/buy decision not yet recorded for this satellite" + part-default chip; `?sat=` absent → "Open this part from a satellite to see the recorded make/buy decision …" + part-default chip. The hard-coded `Decision: MAKE — internal build` / `Decision: BUY — vendor-supplied` rows are gone from the Make/Buy detail side-panel (it now carries only avg build time / completed builds for make, or preferred vendor name/country/ITAR for buy). (3) New top-level `#procurementPanel` (`.panel`, shown only when `mb === 'buy'`) with `renderProcurement(buyCostData)` — hides the MAKE "Build process" + "Materials required" panels for buy; renders PR card(s) (`recent_orders` `kind==='procurement_request'` — material_description, est cost via `costRender.formatMoney`, status, requested date, sat/serial) → VO card(s) (`kind==='vendor_order'` — vendor_name, qty, PO number, lead weeks, status, created date + preferred-vendor country + `ITAR-OK` chip) → PO/invoiced via `costRender.renderBuySheet(buyCostData)`; friendly empty states for no-`?sat=` / no-buy_costs / no-records. `buyCostData` hoisted to the IIFE scope; `renderCostFirstClass` assigns it from the existing `/api/buy-costs` fetch and re-calls `renderProcurement` + `renderWorkflow` so Quote/PO/Invoiced light up once it resolves (no new endpoint, no extra round-trip). (4) `renderWorkflow` rewritten for BUY: step list `Decision · Quote · Purchase request · Vendor order · PO issued · Invoiced` (phantom RFQ/Receiving/Acceptance dropped); `currentIdx` = highest done index across `[!!decision, buy_costs.quoted_unit_cost_usd present, any procurement_request, any vendor_order, any VO.po_number || buy_costs.po_value_usd present, buy_costs.invoiced_value_usd present]`; BUY step pills scroll to `#realizationPanel` (Decision) + `#procurementPanel` (rest); MAKE step list unchanged; no inline `onclick` introduced. <details><summary>Prior position — Plan 31-02 (multi-mesh assembly viewer) COMPLETE</summary>

**Phase 31 IN PROGRESS (2/4 plans) — 3D dimension HUD + clickable assemblies.** Plan 31-02 (Wave 1, autonomous — multi-mesh assembly viewer) — COMPLETE: extended `mount3DViewer(containerEl, partData, opts)` in `turion-space-demo/satellite/satellite-3d.js`. New `export function layoutAssemblyChildren(children) → { ringRoot, childGroups }` near `buildPartMesh` (named, swappable ring↔grid helper): per child row → `buildPartMesh(cd)` (a THREE.Group) → independently scale to `childUnit` (~0.9, shrinks when N>10) → recenter on own origin → place at `theta = (i/N)·2π` on a ring of radius `Math.max(2.2, Math.min(6, childUnit·N/π))` (N===1 → centered) → `g.lookAt(0,y,0)` cosmetic → tag `g.userData.childData = cd` + `g.traverse(o => o.userData.childGroup = g)` → push to `childGroups`. NO parent envelope box (locked decision #2 — the ring IS the assembly; HUD names the parent). `mount3DViewer`: `const isAssembly = Array.isArray(opts.assemblyChildren) && opts.assemblyChildren.length > 0` — when false the EXACT Phase-30 single-mesh block runs (moved verbatim into `if (!isAssembly)`; logic byte-identical, only re-indented; `const safeR/controls` → function-scoped `let`). When true: `layoutAssemblyChildren` → `scene.add(ringRoot)` + widened `GridHelper(12, 24, …)` floor + camera framed off `ringRoot`'s bounding sphere (`camera.position.set(0.8·dist, 0.55·dist, dist)`, `controls.minDistance = safeR·0.4`) + `initialCamPos`/`initialTarget` cached. Raycaster picker (RESEARCH Pattern 3): `THREE.Raycaster` + `THREE.Vector2`; `ndc(ev)` recomputes `renderer.domElement.getBoundingClientRect()` every call (never cached, never `window.innerWidth/innerHeight`); `pickGroup()` = `intersectObjects(childGroups, true)` (recursive — children are Groups of Meshes) → `hits[0].object.userData.childGroup`; `setEmissive(group, on)` traverses the group, saves `{emissive.clone(), emissiveIntensity}` to `m.userData._emSaved` on first hover (skips materials w/o `emissive`), sets `emissive` ← `paletteFor3D(childData.subsystem_code).accent` + `emissiveIntensity = 0.5`, restores on un-hover; cursor `'pointer'` ⇄ `'grab'`. Pointer handlers on `renderer.domElement`: `pointermove` (re-pick, swap hover, skip the selected group so it stays lit), `pointerleave` (un-light hover unless selected, reset cursor), `pointerdown` (record `{x,y}` for the 4px drag-guard), `click` (ignore if moved >4px since pointerdown → orbit drag; else `selectChild(grp)` or `deselect()`). Select/tween (RESEARCH Pattern 4 — `Vector3.lerp` in the RAF loop, NO tween library): `selectChild(grp)` keeps it lit, frames it from its bounding sphere (`d = r/sin(fov/2)·1.6`) approaching from the camera's current direction, sets `tween = { camTo, tgtTo }`, `controls.enabled = false`, fires `opts.onSelect(grp.userData.childData)`; `deselect()` un-lights, `tween = { camTo: initialCamPos, tgtTo: initialTarget }`, `controls.enabled = false`, resets cursor, fires `opts.onSelect(null)` (+ `opts.onDeselect()` if provided); in `tick()` when `tween` active: `camera.position.lerp(tween.camTo, 0.12)` + `controls.target.lerp(tween.tgtTo, 0.12)`, skip `controls.update()` that frame, on convergence (`distanceTo < 0.01` both) `.copy()` the exact targets, `tween = null`, `controls.enabled = true`. Return value always `{ controls, deselect(), selectChild(grp), dispose() }` (leaf `deselect`/`selectChild` are no-ops, so callers can wire a "back to assembly" button unconditionally). `dispose()` adds, assembly path only: `removeEventListener` pointermove/pointerleave/pointerdown/click + `style.cursor = ''`, before the existing (unchanged) `scene.traverse` geometry/material/texture disposal (covers the N child groups since they're in the scene graph) + `renderer.dispose`/`forceContextLoss`/canvas removal. Also added a 10th demo cell to `satellite/3d-test.html` exercising the assembly path (EPS-ASSY with 5 hardcoded children + a "↩ back" button wired to `handle.deselect()`); the existing 9 family cells untouched. Verified: temp-stripped `node --check` on `satellite-3d.js` (top two `import` lines removed) → exit 0; same for `3d-test.html`'s module script → exit 0; `grep -c "assemblyChildren"` 5 (≥2 ✓) / `"getBoundingClientRect"` 1 (≥1 ✓) / `"window.innerWidth\|window.innerHeight"` 0 ✓ / `"intersectObjects(childGroups, true)"` 1 (≥1 ✓) / `"removeEventListener"` 4 (≥3 ✓) / `"deselect"` 9 (≥2 ✓) / `"layoutAssemblyChildren"` 4 / `"@tweenjs\|tween.js\|gsap\|OutlinePass\|EffectComposer\|EdgesGeometry"` 0 ✓; `git diff` shows the leaf single-mesh logic byte-identical (only re-indented) and `chooseTemplate3D`/`normalizeDims`/`perturbForPartNumber`/`paletteFor3D`/`materialFor`/`isWebGLAvailable`/`buildPartMesh` — zero diff lines touch them; Three.js stays `0.184.0` via the existing importmap — no new bare specifiers. Committed only `satellite/satellite-3d.js` + `satellite/3d-test.html` to `turion-space-demo` main as `feat(31-02): multi-mesh assembly viewer (radial ring + raycaster picker + camera tween) in satellite-3d.js` — commit `7b92727`, authored `jeet-avatar <jm@techcloudpro.com>`; NOT pushed (Plan 31-04 owns the push + `deploy-frontend.sh`); the unrelated dirty ERP HTML / `backend/*` / `.superpowers/` left untouched (explicit `-- <files>` form). _Prior position (Plan 30-01, Phase 30 Wave 1, autonomous) — COMPLETE:_ built `turion-space-demo/satellite/satellite-3d.js` (438 lines, 8 exports — `isWebGLAvailable`, `chooseTemplate3D`, `normalizeDims`, `perturbForPartNumber`, `paletteFor3D`, `materialFor`, `buildPartMesh`, `mount3DViewer`) — a reusable Three.js 0.184.0 ES-module part viewer + procedural mesh generator + WebGL feature-detect — plus `satellite/3d-test.html` (149 lines), a standalone 9-cell visual harness covering all 8 `chooseTemplate3D` families. `chooseTemplate3D` ported byte-identical from Phase-27 `generate-cad-svgs.ts` (fastener → assembly → cylindrical → **solar BEFORE plate (W5)** → lens → antenna → plate → subassembly catch-all). `buildPartMesh` → 8 distinct primitive groups (fastener=Cylinder shaft+hex-prism head+thread rings; assembly=Box; cylindrical=Cylinder body+seam rings, THRUSTER→flared nozzle; solar=Box substrate+CanvasTexture cell-grid; lens=stacked Cylinder discs+aperture+accent ring; antenna=Lathe parabola dish+feed boom+horn; plate=thin Box or Torus if RING; subassembly=Box+emissive LED dots+connector pad) — every dim `Math.max`-clamped, mesh normalized to a ~2-unit cube with a 1e-4 zero-guard. `mount3DViewer(containerEl, partData, opts)` → `{ controls, dispose() }` | `null` — Scene/PerspectiveCamera(45)/WebGLRenderer({antialias,alpha}) sized from `containerEl.clientWidth/clientHeight||520` (NOT window), 3-point lighting+ambient, GridHelper floor, camera framed off the bounding sphere, OrbitControls (enableDamping, autoRotate=!!opts.autoRotate), ResizeObserver, RAF loop; returns `null` when WebGL unavailable so callers keep the SVG; `dispose()` = cancelAnimationFrame + ResizeObserver.disconnect + controls.dispose + scene.traverse disposing geometries/materials/textures + renderer.dispose + forceContextLoss + canvas removal. ES module `import * as THREE from 'three'` + `import { OrbitControls } from 'three/addons/controls/OrbitControls.js'` — bare specifiers resolved by the consuming page's import map (added in Plan 30-02); NO bundler, NO npm install in turion-space-demo, NO vendored Three.js. _Prior position (Plan 29-01, Phase 29 Wave 1, autonomous; ran parallel with 29-02) — COMPLETE:_ built `turion-satellite/backend/scripts/audit-satellite-buttons.mjs` (~430 lines, dependency-free Node 20) — a static analyzer that DERIVES the API route allowlist from `backend/src/app.ts`'s mount tree (parses `app.use('/api/...', router)` → resolves the import → each router file's `router.{get,post,patch,put,delete}('<path>', ...)` + nested `router.use('/:x', subRouter)` recursion; normalizes `//`→`/`, strips trailing `/`, keeps `:param` and case) into a Set of `METHOD /api/...` strings (61 routes found). It regex-scans every `satellite/*.html` (recursively incl. `auth/`, skips `cad/`) for: (a) `onclick="..."` attrs — each top-level statement must be an allowlisted built-in (`location.reload()`/`location.href=`/`window.location.*`/`history.back()`/`window.satelliteAuth.signOut()`/`.signInWithMagicLink()`/`document.getElementById('...').remove()`/`.dispatchEvent()`/`.value=`/`dispatchEvent(new Event())`/`event.preventDefault()`/`this.style.*`) OR a call to a function defined in a `<script>` block in the SAME file (`function NAME(` / `const NAME = (` / `NAME = async (` / `window.NAME =`) → else `dead-onclick` violation; (b) `satelliteApi.(get|post|patch)( <expr> )` calls — normalizes the path FAIL-CLOSED: strip `?query`/`#hash` + trailing `/`; template `${...}` collapses its WHOLE segment to `:X`; string-concat `'/a/'+x+'/b'` → each `+...+` boundary is a `:X` segment-fragment BUT 2+ adjacent non-literals (`'/api/'+a+b`) → `unparseable-path`; a bare-identifier arg is resolved against the file text (`const NAME = <expr|ternary>` — both branches; the `function NAME(NAME){...}` wrapper-fn pattern — every `NAME(<literal>)` call site, skipping the definition site) → else `unparseable-path`; then match the candidate `METHOD path` against each route segment-by-segment with `:param`/`:X` wildcards on EITHER side AND EXACT segment-count → miss = `missing-endpoint`. Returns `{routes, onclickCount, apiCallCount, violations}` from a default-exported `async function auditSatelliteButtons({satelliteDir?, backendSrcDir?})` (sibling-repo default `../../../turion-space-demo/satellite`, overridable via `SATELLITE_DIR`/`BACKEND_SRC_DIR`); also a CLI (prints `routes:`/`onclick handlers scanned:`/`satelliteApi calls scanned:`/`violations:` + by-kind, exit 1 on any violation, 2 if a dir is missing). **0 violations against the current codebase** (61 routes, 15 onclick, 57 satelliteApi). Fail-closed verified on a scratch dir: injected `satelliteApi.post('/api/does-not-exist')` → `missing-endpoint` exit 1; `…/instances/${id}/extra` (5 segs) → `missing-endpoint`; `onclick="totallyUndefinedFn()"` → `dead-onclick`; `const tangled='/api/'+segA+foo+bar; satelliteApi.get(tangled)` → `unparseable-path`; missing dir → exit 2. Vitest case at `backend/tests/audit-satellite-buttons.test.ts` (DEVIATION Rule 3: lives in `tests/` not `test/` to match the actual `vitest.config.ts` glob `tests/**/*.test.ts`) — `it.skipIf(!fs.existsSync(satelliteDir))` so CI (which doesn't vendor `turion-space-demo`) stays green while a local `npm test` runs it; asserts `violations.length===0`, `routes.length>20`, `apiCallCount>10`, `onclickCount>0`. `package.json` (both repos): `"audit-buttons": "node scripts/audit-satellite-buttons.mjs"`. Frontend wrapper `turion-space-demo/scripts/audit-satellite-buttons.mjs` = thin re-export (option a): dynamically `import()`s the turion-satellite source-of-truth script with `satelliteDir`→this repo's `./satellite` + `backendSrcDir`→`../turion-satellite/backend/src`; if the sibling backend isn't checked out, prints how to fix it + exits 2 (not a silent pass). `npm run audit-buttons` → 61 routes / 0 violations / exit 0 in both repos. **F4 — `auth/callback.html` reviewed, NO code change**: already (a) runs the Supabase magic-link exchange (`createClient` `detectSessionInUrl:true` + 300 ms wait → `getSession()`), (b) `window.location.replace('/satellite/')` on success, (c) renders `error_description` from the URL hash (or a `'Sign-in failed. The link may have expired.'` fallback) into `#error` + `window.location.replace('/satellite/login.html')` after 3 s on a bad/expired link; `login.html` never carries a `next`/`redirect_to` param so there's nothing to preserve — finding recorded in the SUMMARY. **F3 — `parts.html` honors `?subsystem=`/`?search=`**: inserted a block AFTER the awaited `GET /api/subsystems` populates `#subFilter`'s `<option>`s (synchronously, same IIFE) and BEFORE the first `await load()` — `const qSub = r.getQueryParam('subsystem'); const qSearch = r.getQueryParam('search'); if (qSub && [...sel.options].some(o=>o.value===qSub)) sel.value=qSub; if (qSearch) #searchInput.value=qSearch;` — `load()` reads those values so the table loads pre-filtered; unknown `?subsystem=` harmlessly ignored; no-param path defaults to `''`=all; `?sat=` left alone. Confirmed `cost-render.js`'s `renderRollupRow` emits `parts.html?subsystem=<code>&sat=<id>` — param name matches. **F5 — `instance.html` instance_index>1 hint**: the empty-WO branch (`if (myWos.length === 0)`) now: if `inst.instance_index > 1` AND a sibling with `instance_index===1` for the same `part_definition_id` exists in the already-loaded `allInstances` → renders `Manufacturing & procurement for this part are tracked on instance #1.` + `<a href="/satellite/instance.html?sat=<satId>&id=<sibling.id>">→ Open instance #1</a>`; if no #1 sibling → plain hint text; else (`instance_index===1`) → byte-identical original `<div class="empty"><p>No work orders</p></div>`. Documented pre-existing data state (migs 013/019 only backfill instance #1 — Phase 28 deferred-items #1), not a bug — the hint just explains it to the UAT walker. Pure client-side, no backend call, no new `onclick`/`satelliteApi` — audit still 0 violations. Cost panel / integrations panel / subtree-rollup / `cost_breakdown` source untouched. Backend test suite: 326 pass / 1 skip (pre-existing `supersede.integration.test.ts`) / 0 regressions; the new audit test passes. `node --check` passes on every edited inline `<script>` (parts.html, instance.html, auth/callback.html). ## Accumulated Context

### Roadmap Evolution

- Phase 24 added: Turion Satellite Make/Buy Cost Module — make-cost sheet, buy-cost sheet, make-vs-buy decision records per spec §3.2 (renumbered from 22 due to stale 22- and 23- directories from prior milestones)
- Phase 12 added: Fix Admin Portal UI — Fix broken admin portal screens (restaurants not loading, design issues, mock dashboards), make admin portal production-ready
- Phase 8.1 inserted after Phase 8 (URGENT): Fix rideshare failure paths — no-show fee enforcement, bid race condition, payment failure recovery, no-drivers expiry flow, driver cancel handling

### Decisions

- [Phase 21]: License posture = Option C (hand-rolled ANLZ writer using construct + Deep Symmetry Kaitai spec, no rbox/GPL-3.0 dependency). PDB writer also hand-rolled. Keep existing pyrekordbox-based read path (`rekordbox.py`, `library.py`) as optional import source for users with existing Rekordbox libraries. Native export path must work end-to-end with zero Rekordbox install on the host machine.
- [Phase 21-01]: content_id prefixing scheme for imported_tracks — 'import_<sha1(abs_path)[:16]>' for folder scans, 'rbximport_<rb_content_id>' for Rekordbox-bridge. Two origins coexist without schema extension. Idempotence via UNIQUE(file_path) + INSERT OR IGNORE.
- [Phase 21-01]: WAVE_FORMAT_EXTENSIBLE detection — flag, don't drop. Warning surfaced in POST response warnings[] per-file; CDJ-3000 may refuse these but we let the user decide whether to re-encode.
- [Phase 21-01]: StateDB _DEFAULT_PATH changed to late-bound (read in __init__ at call time, not captured as function default) so tests can monkeypatch the state DB location without touching the user's real state.db. Existing tests unaffected.
- [Phase 21-03]: ANLZ writer built with construct>=2.10 (MIT) only. Zero rbox imports. `test_no_rbox_imported` regression guard installed. Plan's struct constants were verified wrong (PWAV 400 not 800, PWV3 1 byte/column not 6, PPTH len is byte count not char count) — fixed by direct inspection of `/Volumes/Untitled/PIONEER/USBANLZ/P001/00000019/ANLZ0000.DAT`.
- [Phase 21-03]: .2EX writer ships as PMAI+PPTH stub only. PWV6/PWV7/PWVC/XWVv bodies are undocumented and deferred to a later polish task within Phase 21. CDJ-3000 falls back to .EXT waveform when .2EX is minimal.
- [Phase 21-03]: Level 1 oracle is scoped to the core tags our writer emits (PPTH/PQTZ/PWAV/PCOB). PVBR (MP3 VBR index) and PWV2 (second detail waveform) are present in every reference .DAT but are documented deferred scope — their absence is NOT a Level 1 failure. Level 3 byte-equivalence is 0/20 today as expected; Phase 21 exit gate is ≥50%.
- [Phase 21-03]: Round-trip adapter (`anlz_to_writer_input`) routes duplicate PCOB tags by `cue_type` field, not positional order, so writer output is tolerant of reorderings. `_parse_anlz_tags_all` added to support duplicate-magic walking (plan originally specified only dict-valued `_parse_anlz_tags` which would silently drop one PCOB).
- [Phase 21-04]: pyrekordbox cannot parse PDB — its `db6` module reads master.db (SQLCipher), not export.pdb. Plan's tests called `Rekordbox6Database(export.pdb)` which would raise. Rule 1 bug fix: built `pdb_reader.py` as a companion parser from the same construct Structs as pdb_writer. Writer+reader form a symmetric matched set validated by round-trip through the real 1438-track reference export.
- [Phase 21-04]: exportLibrary.db is SQLCipher-encrypted (header `0d5ad2b9304f8768...`), NOT plain SQLite as the plan assumed. pyrekordbox master.db key + `pioneer`/`onelibrary`/zeros all fail with "file is not a database". Rule 4 architectural deviation: module deferred, returns `{status:"deferred"}` and writes no file. CDJ-3000X falls back to export.pdb when OneLibrary is missing; CDJ-3000 (primary Phase 21 target) ignores it entirely.
- [Phase 21-04]: exportExt.pdb written as 9 empty structural tables with strange_marker=0x03EC and page_flags=0x64 matching the reference layout. Empty rows make CDJ-3000X fall back to export.pdb for content — which is what MixMind v1 wants without dual-maintaining two data sources. Populated-row emission is a Phase 21 polish follow-up.
- [Phase 21-04]: djprofile.nxs deliberately NOT written — reference file contains the original owner's name (PII). Anonymised profile requires a product decision (default/per-user/consent flow) and is explicitly deferred.
- [Phase 21-04]: Aux file (RBFLTR/DEVSETTING/MYSETTING/MYSETTING2/DJMMYSETTING) bytes embedded as SINGLE-LINE base64 constants. A multi-line concatenated literal dropped a 0x20 space byte inside RBFLTR.DAT during dev (manifested as 231B vs expected 232B). All 5 files now SHA256-match reference.
- [Phase 21-04]: TrackRow struct size is 98 bytes (construct.sizeof()), not 92 as initially assumed. Fixed with verified-at-runtime comment; caught when row_offsets started pointing inside the previous row.
- [Phase 21-04]: test_no_rbox_imported rewritten to walk `ast.parse(source)` for `ast.Import`/`ast.ImportFrom` nodes instead of substring search — docstring prose mentioning "import rbox" as a prohibition no longer false-positives.
- [Phase 21-06]: SLOTS_PER_BUCKET = 20 with GLOBAL 1-indexed slot numbering (plan said 38 per-bucket-reset). Reference USB enumeration: bucket 00001 holds slots 1..19, 00002..00045 hold 20 each, 00046 is partial tail (900..908). Rule 1 deviation — the plan's 38 was a misreading of the handoff.
- [Phase 21-06]: artwork_id = global_slot directly (single-field Int32ul key). No bit-packing. bucket = slot // 20 + 1 is deterministic from slot alone.
- [Phase 21-06]: PDB Artwork table (page_type 9) stays empty — reference `/Volumes/Untitled/PIONEER/rekordbox/export.pdb` has `row_count(9) == 0`. CDJ firmware reconstructs JPEG path from Track row artwork_id directly. Avoids reverse-engineering a currently-undocumented row format.
- [Phase 21-06]: Pillow quality=85 + optimize=True + progressive=False — empirically within [0.5x, 2x] file-size band of Pioneer reference JPEGs, MAE < 10 on round-trip. Oracle test with 15 sampled slots passes all tolerance bars.
- [Phase 21-06]: Mutagen (GPL-2.0) already in requirements from 21-01; 21-06 only extends its usage to APIC/PICTURE/covr extraction. Commercial-distribution license audit deferred to dedicated pre-launch phase.
- [Phase 21-06]: Some reference USB artwork files have non-0xFFD8FF headers (observed bucket 00003/49-51, 00005/93 — likely Pioneer-internal obfuscation). Oracle `_is_real_jpeg()` guard filters them; our writer only emits standard JPEG SOI files.
- [Phase 24-03]: Cost-table supersede uses single CTE; decisions-table supersede uses 3-step BEGIN/COMMIT transaction. Reason: Postgres checks partial UNIQUE indexes per-row at INSERT and partial indexes are NOT deferrable. The single CTE on make_buy_decisions (which has `uq_make_buy_decisions_current WHERE superseded_by IS NULL`) would briefly hold two NULL-superseded rows mid-statement and the new INSERT would collide with the existing live row. The 3-step transaction (UPDATE old.superseded_by=old.id → INSERT new with NULL → UPDATE old.superseded_by=new.id) has identical atomicity (BEGIN/COMMIT) and avoids the constraint conflict. Cost tables (make_costs, buy_costs) deliberately omit the partial UNIQUE per 24-01 design, so the CTE works there as written.
- [Phase 24-03]: HARD GATE message is the verbatim CONTEXT.md decision #8 string `Make-vs-buy decision required and must be "buy" before procurement`. Tested with `expect(res.body.error).toBe(GATE_MSG)` strict equality — any future drift caught immediately. Gate applies to both POST /api/satellites/:satId/procurement-requests and POST /api/satellites/:satId/vendor-orders.
- [Phase 24-03]: Audit log inserts are non-fatal (wrapped in inner try/catch with console.warn). The user-visible action — recording a decision, rate change, fx seed — must NOT fail because of a logging-side issue. Audit table has no FK constraints on actor_user_id (Supabase auth users are off-schema); failures are unlikely; the safety net is for future schema drift.

- [Phase quick-337]: Used relative imports throughout validators package to allow tests/validators/ suite to run without sys.path manipulation
- [Phase 37-02]: Atomic per-batch transaction (single BEGIN/COMMIT) with per-row error isolation — mapping throws / dup-id checks / source-not-found push into errors[] and CONTINUE the loop; only a FATAL throw outside the per-row try (DB connection drop, unexpected pool error) triggers ROLLBACK. Whole-batch atomicity preserved at the run level (migration_runs row is inside the same txn as the qb_records.status flips and ns_table INSERTs), but partial-batch is intentional: a single bad row doesn't kill the rest. Matches plan §truths.
- [Phase 37-02]: Cross-record refs (Item→COA, Invoice→Customer+Item, Bill→Vendor+COA) resolve via the SAME pool client passed into applyMapping — so a batch migrating customers BEFORE invoices in one POST sees the in-flight customer rows. Resolving outside the txn would miss them; using a fresh `query()` call would too.
- [Phase 37-02]: Unresolved refs → __warnings array on the ns row (best-effort + diagnostic), NOT errors[]. Plan §truths explicitly says "row is still inserted". Unresolved means the resolver returned the bare qb_id with no ' · ' name separator.
- [Phase 37-02]: turion.customers gets a SPECIAL-CASED INSERT shape `(id, name, source_data)` because the table has a denormalized name column (matches the existing keyedEntity and salesforce.ts patterns). Every other ns table is plain `(id, source_data)`. The migrate handler branches on `if (nsTable === 'customers')` to write the name explicitly.
- [Phase 37-02]: Customer ref resolution targets `turion.customers.name` (denormalized column), NOT `source_data->>'DisplayName'` — the denormalized name is what migrate writes, what keyedEntity reads, what salesforce.ts uses. Vendor + gl_account resolvers target `source_data->>'name'` (those tables have no denormalized name column).
- [Phase 37-02]: Bill paymentRecorded.method hardcoded 'ACH' (vs invoice's PaymentMethod from source) — seed bills do not carry a PaymentMethod field; ACH is the demo's default for AP. Invoices read source's PaymentMethod directly when present.
- [Phase 37-02]: Ramp double-prefix id `RMP-RMP-TXN-44012` (seed ramp_ids already start with `RMP-`) is plan-spec-compliant per the verify grep `id: \`RMP-`. Distinguishes QB-migrated bills (id = QB DocNumber) from Ramp-migrated bills (id = `RMP-<ramp_id>`).
- [Phase 37-02]: Exhaustive type guard via `const _exhaustive: never = t` at the end of applyMapping — tsc fails if a 7th QbType ever lands without a branch. Prefers compile-time over runtime throw.
- [Phase 37-02]: turion.ramp_card_txns has no `updated_at` column (only `qb_records` does) — the Ramp UPDATE drops `updated_at=now()`; QB UPDATE keeps it. Caught during implementation (Rule 3 — would have thrown at first POST).
